# Prediction Calibration Reference — July 9, 2026 (Scored June 1, 2026)

Use this document when analyzing Predictive History lectures to accurately assess prediction statuses.

> **Daily update — July 9, 2026 (Day 133).** **The war reached its sharpest RHETORICAL peak since the MOU — and the execution still stayed on the AIR/MISSILE and MARITIME rungs; every US GROUND indicator remained at zero.** On **July 8 US CENTCOM announced it had "started conducting additional strikes against Iran to further degrade their ability to threaten freedom of navigation in the Strait of Hormuz"** — a second wave on top of the July 7 package, with reporting putting the campaign at **80+ targets and more than 60 IRGC small boats** struck, alongside air-defense, command-and-control, coastal-radar, surveillance and anti-ship-missile sites. From the **NATO Ankara summit, Trump declared the ceasefire and MOU "over"** (**"a waste of time"; Iran "vicious, violent"; "every time they hit us, we hit them 20"**) and **threatened to bomb and blockade Iran again** — then **walked it back the same day** (**"I don't think it's going to start again"**), saying the exchange would not lead to long-term military action. **Brent JUMPED 5.2% to settle at $78.02/bbl** (WTI up in step), easing off session highs on the walk-back — still far below the **$130+ a US ground commitment would imply.** **GROUND-INVASION RUNG UNCHANGED AT PEAK RHETORIC:** even with the ceasefire declared "over" and the largest post-MOU strike wave, there were **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft;** the **blockade stays lifted (Day 21).** Standing posture unchanged (**~50,000 troops, ~24 warships** — carriers **Abraham Lincoln** and **George H.W. Bush**, 15 destroyers; **USS Boxer ARG / 11th MEU** and **USS Tripoli ARG / 31st MEU** in the Arabian Sea). **It is Iran, not the US, using force at the chokepoint — the structural inverse of the predicted US seizure.** On **July 9 Khamenei was buried at the Imam Reza shrine in Mashhad**, his birthplace, closing the six-day state funeral; new Supreme Leader **Mojtaba Khamenei** still had not appeared publicly. The **imminent-US-ground-invasion / Hormuz-and-Kharg-seizure thesis stays disconfirmed-and-inverted** — this is the moment a genuinely imminent invasion would show in force posture, and it did not. (Al Jazeera live; NBC News; PBS NewsHour; CNBC; Britannica; Wikipedia "State funeral of Ali Khamenei", July 8-9, 2026.)

humanitarian-goods arrangement,** which **US officials said was not finalized**), a **communications channel to flag and address ceasefire violations,** and an **agreed focus on restoring Hormuz and Isfahan sites,** offering only **Bushehr and Tehran**). **Trump:** "denuclearisation of Iran is moving along well" / **"I think they've come a long way";** **VP Vance** said nuclear issues would > **Daily update — June 30, 2026 (Day 124).** **The three-day breach-retaliation cycle BROKE — on June 29 the US and Iran agreed to STAND DOWN and Trump announced a fresh round of talks in Doha for Tuesday June 30 — routing the contest back off the ground rung and into diplomacy.** A **US official said both sides would "stand down"** after trading strikes over the weekend and that **vessels would move freely through the Strait of Hormuz** ("no drones or missiles launched by Iran at US assets in Bahrain and Kuwait last night reached their targets"). **Trump posted that "Iran has requested a meeting" and that "it will take place tomorrow in Doha"** — talks originally set for Switzerland, **moved to Qatar with the topic re-scoped to the Hormuz standoff;** press secretary **Karoline Leavitt said special envoy Steve Witkoff and Jared Kushner would fly to Doha for "high-level meetings"** held alongside "technical talks." **Iran's deputy FM and senior negotiator Kazem Gharibabadi denied any technical talks had been scheduled "for this week,"** leaving initial uncertainty over Iranian participation. **Iran and Oman held their first meeting on managing the Strait** (disagreements persist over corridor management and demining); **Iran said it would demine alone and rejected international involvement** (including a **France-Oman joint demining initiative**), calling the situation **"sensitive and complex,"** while **Oman and the GCC rejected Iranian transit tolls** as "not acceptable." **Iran's president said Tehran received "$6 billion out of the total $12 billion of Iranian resources in Qatar"** under the MOU (reportedly earmarked for US wheat and corn). **Oil eased: Brent rose only slightly to ~$69.70/bbl** off a four-month low (Trump: prices "charging down"); **Hormuz traffic stayed thinned** (29 vessels Saturday, 12 Sunday, down from 70 the prior Wednesday) but **major container lines resumed Gulf services** and the **first commercial flight from Tehran landed in Dubai.** On the **Lebanon track, the Lebanese army chief held talks with a US military commander** on security. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED and de-escalatory on the US side** — the day's US move was **diplomatic** (a stand-down plus the Doha meeting), not a ground operation; the **blockade stays lifted (Day 12),** the **USS Boxer ARG stays disaggregated,** and there are **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft.** The cycle resolved back into negotiation; the 60-day MOU remains in force but strained, and the live risk is **deal durability — now whether the Doha meeting actually convenes** given the Gharibabadi-vs-Trump contradiction — in the diplomatic/air/missile/maritime register, **not a forming ground invasion** (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: iran-us-deal-reached-june-2026; iran-hormuz-administration-tolls-dispute-june-2026). (NPR; CNBC; NBC News; CBS News; Euronews; The Times of Israel; The National; TradingEconomics, June 29, 2026.)

> **Daily update — June 28, 2026 (Day 122).** **The breach-retaliation cycle repeated for a second straight day — but it again ran on the AIR/MARITIME rung, not the ground one.** Early on **June 27 an Iranian one-way attack drone hit the Panama-flagged tanker M/T Kiku** (~2 million barrels of Qatari crude bound for Fujairah, UAE; **bridge damaged, all crew safe, no spill**) as it transited near the Strait of Hormuz — the second Iranian strike on shipping in three days. For the **second consecutive night the US retaliated with a fresh, LARGER round of airstrikes on Iran** — a senior US official said **"it's a larger strike than last night,"** and **CENTCOM said the strikes hit Iranian air-defense and surface-to-air missile systems, drone storage, cruise missiles, targeting radars and minelayer capabilities** ("U.S. forces remain vigilant, lethal, and ready"). **VP Vance** repeated **"violence will be met with violence... Iran signed a ceasefire agreement. We have honored it."** **Iran cast the US as the violator:** **Mohsen Rezaei** warned any breach would draw a **"swift and decisive"** response; the **IRGC** said the US "attacked the coasts of the Islamic Republic" over a ship on "an unauthorized route"; and the **IRGC Navy warned future US attacks would draw "a broader response than this."** **Bahrain condemned an Iranian drone strike as a violation of its sovereignty,** and **Bahrain, the UAE, Kuwait and the GCC** condemned Iran's drone attacks on shipping. On the **Lebanon track, Hezbollah rejected the June 26 Israel-Lebanon framework as "null and void,"** and an Israeli strike on **Nabatieh killed one.** **Oil stayed in de-escalation pricing:** **Brent fell below $73/bbl** on June 26 (lowest since Feb 27; **an over-10% weekly drop, the largest in a month**), with each strike producing only a brief rebound, as **Gulf exports recovered to ~75% of pre-war** (~4.8M bpd through Hormuz, the highest since the war began) and **Saudi Arabia resumed loading at Ras Tanura;** the **US-Navy Joint Maritime Information Center (JMIC) announced a widened Hormuz transit route near Oman,** and **Trump confirmed the Strait remains open.** **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED** — for the second day running the **US answered an Iranian maritime strike with AIR power, not ground forces;** the **blockade stays lifted (Day 10),** the **USS Boxer ARG stays disaggregated,** and there are **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft.** The 60-day MOU remains technically in force but increasingly strained; the live risk is whether the day-on-day breach-retaliation cycle keeps repeating in the air/missile/maritime register, not a forming ground invasion (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: iran-us-deal-reached-june-2026; iran-hormuz-administration-tolls-dispute-june-2026; iran-nuclear-inspections-dispute-june-2026; kharg-seizure-total-control-threat-june-2026). Roger Wicker:** the MOU **"negotiates away the victories of Operation Epic Fury."** **Venezuela:** the Maduro/Flores hearing stays **postponed June 30 → July 22.** **Russia-Ukraine:** grinding war; next Istanbul round window **June 20-30.** **US-China:** trade truce holds, Taiwan the flashpoint. **DPRK:** no new launch. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED and still DE-ESCALATORY** — the postponement is a **diplomatic wobble** and the Lebanon flare-up a **contained air/naval risk,** neither a ground move; **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft;** the only US escalation levers remain **air-power** ("restart the bombing") and **diplomatic** ("no tolls or no deal"). > **Daily update — June 19, 2026 (Day 113).** **The war-ending deal moved from announced to CONSUMMATED.** **Trump and Iranian President Pezeshkian signed the MOU electronically Wednesday night** (Trump at a **candlelit G7 dinner outside Paris,** Pezeshkian remotely) and it **took immediate effect,** **cancelling the in-person Friday June 19 Geneva ceremony** (teams still convene at **Bürgenstock** Friday for a **60-day nuclear/sanctions window**). **US Central Command announced the naval blockade of Iranian ports has been LIFTED** ("in accordance with the president's direction"; military vessels remain only to monitor compliance), and **the Strait of Hormuz began reopening** — **~18 vessels transited June 17-18 (Windward), the first transit in ~110 days (Lloyd's List)** — via the **northern (Iranian) and southern (Omani) routes** while **~80 mines on the central channel** are cleared (CNBC: full normalization **"weeks"** away). **VP Vance** said **"12.5 million barrels of oil passed through... last night"** (a war-era high) but warned **"no tolls or no deal,"** colliding with **Iran's signal it will "charge fees for services"** at the strait **jointly with Oman.** **Brent held a three-month-plus low (~$77).** **Lebanon stays the live fault line:** **Netanyahu** said Israel will **"not withdraw from"** its southern-Lebanon security zone, **Iran's FM** said that **violates** the deal, and **Hezbollah** claimed it **repelled Israeli advances** Thursday (Lebanon toll **~3,912 killed / 11,873 wounded** since March 2). The administration **briefed congressional leaders** Thursday (GOP Sen. **Wicker:** the deal **"negotiates away"** the military's victories). **Russia-Ukraine:** the G7 Ukraine statement and UK "shadow fleet" sanctions carried forward; next Istanbul round still proposed **June 20-30.** **Venezuela:** the Maduro/Flores hearing is **postponed June 30 → July 22;** Rodriguez holds the presidency; Cuba's crisis persists. **US-China:** trade truce holds, Taiwan the flashpoint. **DPRK:** no new launch after the June 8-9 Xi-Kim summit. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED and now DECISIVELY DE-ESCALATORY / near-falsified** — the **blockade is physically lifted, US forces are to withdraw, and Hormuz is reopening by negotiated mine-clearing,** the executed inverse of the predicted seizure; **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no draft;** the only US escalation levers are **air-power** ("restart the bombing") and **diplomatic** ("no tolls or no deal"). Residual contestation is about **durability,** not existence — the still-mined central route, the toll/fee dispute, and the unresolved Lebanon track — but a collapse over any would return the war to the **air/naval/missile register, not vindicate a ground invasion** (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: iran-us-deal-reached-june-2026; iran-deal-israel-lebanon-withdrawal-june-2026; kharg-seizure-total-control-threat-june-2026). > **Daily update — June 18, 2026 (Day 112).** **The war-ending deal survived the close of the G7, but two questions kept it short of consummation: an unreleased text and a hardening Lebanon dispute.** **Trump wrapped his final day at the G7 in the French Alps (June 17)** with a news conference defending what he branded **"the Trump deal"** — a **14-point agreement** he says commits Iran to **"never"** having a nuclear weapon and to reopening the **Strait of Hormuz** — and warned that if Iran does not abide, the United States **"may restart its bombing campaign"** (an **air-power threat, not a ground one**). **G7 allies endorsed the framework** (French President **Macron: "a very good deal"** that "puts a stop to a situation of great instability"), yet **no full MOU text had been released publicly** days after the "within 24-48 hours" pledge; officials continue to describe the **~page-and-a-half** document as essentially **"a commitment to negotiate,"** opening a **second phase of nuclear talks** with **no stated plan yet for Iran's nuclear capabilities.** The **in-person signing stayed set for Friday June 19 in Switzerland (Bürgenstock/Luzern), with VP Vance** representing the US. **Lebanon hardened:** Iran's FM **Araghchi** reiterated the deal requires an **Israeli withdrawal from Lebanon;** a US official says it does not; **Netanyahu** pledged to hold **"security zones";** and **Israeli ministers said Israel "won't be bound" by the Iran deal** and threatened to hit Hezbollah with **"full force."** **Oil fell a fourth straight session** — **Brent ~$79/bbl Wednesday** (a three-month low) — on expected supply returns (Friday signing, higher OPEC+ quotas, increased UAE output). **Hormuz remained closed in practice:** **ADNOC CEO Sultan Al Jaber said the strait is still not open** because Iran is conditioning traffic, with **~230 loaded oil tankers waiting inside the Gulf.** **Russia-Ukraine:** the G7 issued a **joint statement** pledging renewed Ukraine support (air-defense, per Zelensky) and Russia pressure that **Trump signed onto;** the **UK unveiled dozens of new sanctions** on Russia's **"shadow fleet."** **Venezuela:** Maduro+Flores's next NYC court date was **postponed from June 30 to July 22** (FIFA World Cup logistics); Rodriguez holds the presidency; Cuba's crisis persists. **US-China:** trade truce holds, Taiwan the flashpoint. **DPRK:** no new launch beyond the June 8-9 Xi visit and Kim's June 4 enrichment-plant reveal; US/Japan/SK reaffirmed denuclearization (June 12). **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED / DECISIVELY DE-ESCALATORY** — no US troops in Iran, no Kharg ground assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft; the lone US escalation threat aired at the G7 was a resumption of **bombing,** not a ground push, and the MOU **withdraws US forces and lifts the blockade,** the structural inverse of the predicted seizure. > **Daily update — June 16, 2026 (Day 110).** **The war-ending deal firmed toward consummation.** On **June 15,** VP **JD Vance** disclosed on ABC's *Good Morning America* that the memorandum of understanding had **already been "digitally" signed on Sunday June 14** by **Trump, Vance and Iran's Parliament Speaker Mohammad Baqer Qalibaf** (per a senior US official) — narrowing the announce-vs-consummate gap that had defined ~39 prior slipped timelines — though the **in-person ceremony remains set for Friday June 19 in Geneva** (Vance + Qalibaf) and Trump said the **full text** would be released **"some time after Friday."** Trump said Supreme Leader **Mojtaba Khamenei had approved** the deal (**"I understand the answer is yes"**) and that it was endorsed **"in concept and great detail"** by the US, Israel, Saudi Arabia, the UAE, Qatar and others. **Iran's Supreme National Security Council confirmed a finalized 14-point MOU** (Mehr): immediate/permanent cessation of hostilities on all fronts **including Lebanon;** complete **naval-blockade lifting within 30 days;** US **force withdrawal** from around Iran; **Hormuz reopening;** oil-sales-sanctions suspension; a **nuclear agreement within 60 days;** and release of **~$24B** in frozen assets during talks. **Oil hit a three-month low:** WTI **-4.8% to ~$80.75** and Brent **-4.7% to ~$83.17** at Monday's close (lowest since the first week of March), while **Asian/global equities soared** (Nikkei **+5.5%,** Kospi **+5.7%,** Taiex **+2.7%**). Analysts cautioned the relief would be **gradual** — the strait reopens for **mine removal only after Friday's signing,** and full normalization could take **"months"** (Ringbakken) to **"over a year"** (Anchan). **Still strained by Lebanon:** Israel's June 14 Beirut/Dahieh strike **killed at least three,** Netanyahu (June 15) said he had **"saved Israel from annihilation"** and insisted he retains **"freedom of operation"** against Hezbollah, **Israeli ministers said Israel won't be bound** by the Iran deal ("if Iran strikes, we'll hit it with full force"), Iran's **Qalibaf warned the strikes threaten the deal,** and **Trump warned Netanyahu not to "blow it."** **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED / DECISIVELY DE-ESCALATORY** — no US troops in Iran, no Kharg ground assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft; the MOU **withdraws US forces and lifts the blockade,** the structural inverse of the predicted seizure. The de-escalation is **contingent on the Friday ceremony and the actual Hormuz reopening holding** (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: iran-us-deal-reached-june-2026; kharg-seizure-total-control-threat-june-2026). (The Times of Israel; Al Jazeera; NPR; NBC News; PBS NewsHour; OilPrice.com; Jerusalem Post; Fox News; CNBC, June 14-15, 2026.)

> **Daily update — June 15, 2026 (Day 109).** **The US and Iran reached a deal to end the war.** On **Sunday June 14,** President Trump and Pakistani Prime Minister **Shehbaz Sharif** jointly announced the agreement and **Iranian state media broadcast it;** Sharif posted that a peace deal **"has been REACHED"** and that **"both sides have declared the immediate and permanent termination of military operations on all fronts, including in Lebanon."** Trump wrote that the deal is **"now complete,"** that he **"hereby fully authorize[s] the toll free opening of the Strait of Hormuz,"** and authorized the **immediate removal of the US naval blockade** (**"Ships of the World, start your engines. Let the oil flow!"**). **Reported MOU terms:** a **60-day ceasefire extension;** Hormuz **reopened without tolls** (Iran clears its own mines, potentially into mid-2027); the US **lifts the blockade and withdraws forces from the Gulf region;** a **15-20 year Iranian enrichment lockout** with nuclear-site dismantling; and **staggered release of ~$25B** in frozen assets tied to compliance (Trump: **"no money"** until Iran performs). A **formal signing** was set for **Friday June 19 in Switzerland (Geneva),** to be executed electronically by Trump or in person by **VP JD Vance.** **Oil plunged:** WTI fell **>4.5% to ~$80** and Brent **~4% to ~$83** as Sunday-evening trading opened — both their **lowest since early March.** **BUT IT IS NOT YET CONSUMMATED:** the text is **unsigned until Friday,** Supreme Leader **Mojtaba Khamenei's** approval was **unconfirmed,** and earlier on June 14 Iran's **Fars/Foreign Ministry had denied an "approved text" existed.** It is **already strained by the Lebanon track:** Israel struck a **Hezbollah command center in Beirut's Dahieh** and killed a **senior Hezbollah commander** in southern Lebanon around the announcement (Trump: the Beirut attack **"should not have happened... at a time when we are so close to a Peace Deal"**); **Netanyahu told Trump Israel would not consider itself bound** to halt Lebanon operations, and some Hormuz tankers reportedly paused. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED / DECISIVELY DE-ESCALATORY** — the deal **reopens Hormuz by negotiation and withdraws US forces,** the structural inverse of the predicted ground-invasion / resource-seizure; no US troops in Iran, no Kharg ground assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft. This is the **cleanest disconfirmation yet** of Predictive History's imminent-ground-invasion / Hormuz-and-Kharg-seizure thesis — contingent on the Friday signing holding (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: iran-us-deal-reached-june-2026; kharg-seizure-total-control-threat-june-2026; iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026). > **Daily update — June 14, 2026 (Day 108).** The war reached its **scheduled signing day — still unratified by Tehran.** On **Saturday June 13** Trump posted that the deal was **"scheduled to get signed tomorrow [Sunday June 14], and immediately after it is signed, the Hormuz Strait is OPEN TO ALL,"** calling it a guarantee of **"long-term peace in the region"** and **"A WALL TO NO NUCLEAR WEAPON,"** with Iran that **"no longer want[s] a Nuclear Weapon, nor will they have one."** Two new wrinkles emerged: Trump said **"no money will exchange hands"** (cutting against the reported **$24B** frozen-assets release Iran had made a precondition) and that the US **"will go in and get the Nuclear Dust, buried deep under the powerful sunken granite mountains"** at an undetermined later time **"when all is calm,"** to be down-blended and destroyed. The signing was set for **Europe (reported as Geneva)** with **VP JD Vance + Witkoff + Kushner** attending (Trump not attending); **four US Air Force C-17s** had departed to Europe to move equipment for Vance's travel. **BUT IT WAS UNSIGNED AND UNRATIFIED:** a senior US official said the US was **not "100%"** confident it would be signed; Iran's Foreign Ministry said Tehran had **"not yet reached a final decision";** Supreme Leader **Mojtaba Khamenei's** approval was **unconfirmed** even as lower-level officials signed off — against a record in which Trump had called a deal imminent **~39 times.** **Oil:** Brent held its **~2-month low in the high-$80s** (WTI ~$88) on signing optimism; the US **energy secretary said Hormuz ship traffic was increasing.** **Lebanon:** cumulative **~3,700 killed** since March 2 as Israeli strikes on Tyre/the south continued under the full-city evacuation order. **Russia-Ukraine:** static (Pokrovsk hottest; next Istanbul June 20-30). **Venezuela:** Maduro+Flores next NYC court date June 30; Rodriguez holds the presidency; Cuba's crisis persists. **US-China:** trade truce holds (Taiwan flashpoint; June 11 60-day tariff pause). **DPRK:** Xi-Kim "strategic cooperation" pledge stands. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED / DE-ESCALATORY** — the only US movement was C-17s carrying **signing-ceremony equipment** to Europe (NOT a combat deployment); the reported MOU would **WITHDRAW US forces and LIFT the blockade;** no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft. the Iranians **"very dishonorable people to deal with"**), while **Iran's Mehr** said final talks **won't begin until half the $24B is released and the blockade ends;** **Netanyahu said Israel is not a party.** The war also **stayed kinetic:** overnight June 11-12 **Iranian drones fired on Indian commercial shipping** leaving Hormuz and **US forces downed two Iranian drones** (Trump: **"totally unacceptable"**) — no US KIA. **Oil:** Brent **fell toward ~$88.40-$88.65/bbl** — its **lowest in nearly two months** — and the US pump average eased to **~$4.11/gal.** **Lebanon:** cumulative toll since March 2 **~3,700 killed** (Israeli losses 29 soldiers + 1 contractor); > **Daily update — June 12, 2026 (Day 106).** The war's **sharpest US-Iran kinetic cycle to date erupted and then reversed within roughly a day.** The trigger was the **downing of a US Apache helicopter in the Strait of Hormuz** (~June 9; **both pilots rescued uninjured;** Washington blamed Tehran), which the US used as the predicate for **two consecutive nights of strikes on Iran:** June 9 hit **Qeshm Island and the ports of Sirik, Jask and Bandar Abbas** (Iran reported destroyed water reservoirs and damaged telecoms), and **June 10 brought a ~4-hour barrage from ~22:15 GMT of about 49 Tomahawk cruise missiles** across Iran — **some ~40 miles from Tehran** — striking surveillance/communications/air-defense sites at **Karaj and Hormuz-area ports** (Trump: *"We were really close to a deal. But they keep tapping us along"*). Iran's **IRGC declared the Strait of Hormuz "closed until further notice" to all shipping** and barraged US bases in **Bahrain (Sheikh Isa), Kuwait (Ali Al Salem + Ahmad Al-Jaber) and Jordan (Al-Azraq, 12 ballistic missiles),** with Tasnim claiming **"eighteen important targets"** destroyed and **two oil tankers reportedly hit;** Bahrain's interceptions **injured an 11-year-old girl,** Jordan downed ~20 missiles with no casualties, southern Iran reported ≥2 wounded at Kargan. On **June 11 Trump posted the war's most explicit territorial-seizure threat** — *"we will be taking Kharg Island, and other oil infrastructure points, and assume total control of their Oil and Gas Markets, much like we have with Venezuela"* — and vowed to hit Iran **"VERY HARD TONIGHT."** Then, **within hours, he reversed:** he told Fox News he was **"not sure America has the stomach"** to take Kharg, **cancelled the threatened Thursday-night strikes** (citing talks taken to Iran's **"highest level"** and approved), and announced **"a great settlement of the war with Iran"** that is **"subject to finalization of documents"** with a likely **signing "maybe in Europe"** within days; reported terms track the **60-day MOU** (Hormuz reopened, no tolls, Iran clears mines, US lifts blockade + sanctions waivers). **CRITICAL CAVEATS: Iran did NOT confirm any deal, and Netanyahu said Israel is not a party.** **Oil: Brent sank ~4.2% to ~$89.15/bbl** — its first move below $90 this cycle — on the de-escalation. **Lebanon:** Israeli strikes killed **~16 across the south June 9-10** (Tayr Debba 9; Deir Qanoun en-Nahr 3; a mosque/clinic at Deir ez-Zahrani 3; Tyre city 1); cumulative toll since March 2 **~3,696 killed / ~11,413 wounded;** **UN's Volker Turk** is sending **investigators next week** to assess violations of international law by all parties. **Russia-Ukraine:** static (Pokrovsk hottest; next Istanbul round June 20-30). **Venezuela:** Maduro+Flores next NYC court date June 30; Rodriguez holds the presidency. **US-China:** trade truce holds. **DPRK:** Xi-Kim "strategic cooperation" pledge stands. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED IN EXECUTION** — no US troops in Iran, no Kharg ground assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no Guard activation, no draft — **but DECLARED INTENT reached its highest point of the war (the explicit Kharg-seizure / "total control" statement) before being RETRACTED the same day;** the active instruments stayed the air/naval strike package and the blockade, and the day's dominant resolution was a pivot back to a negotiated reopening of Hormuz (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: kharg-seizure-total-control-threat-june-2026; > **Daily update — June 9, 2026 (Day 103).** The cycle's most serious escalation **climaxed and then de-escalated within ~24 hours.** Following Iran's June 7 barrage, **Israel struck Iranian territory for the first time since the April 8 ceasefire** — hitting **Tehran, Isfahan, Karaj and Tabriz** early June 8 with **air-launched ballistic missiles,** plus a **petrochemical plant at Mahshahr (Khuzestan)** and **truck-based surface-to-air missile launchers** (Times of Israel; Al Jazeera; ABC News, June 8). **Iran fired ~30 ballistic missiles in total since Sunday night,** its IRGC branding the campaign **"Operation Nasr" ("Victory")** and claiming strikes on **two Israeli military bases;** most were intercepted, **debris reached Jordan and the West Bank,** and **no casualties were confirmed from the exchange itself** (the only deaths remain the June 7 Beirut strike: 2 killed, ~20 wounded). **Trump intervened directly to block a larger Israeli strike** prepared for late June 8 — **"I made Netanyahu stop," "I call the shots,"** telling Netanyahu **"we are close to doing something good in terms of a deal"** (MS NOW; Arab News; Financial Times, June 8). By **June 8-9 both sides halted:** Iran's military announced **"the cessation of armed forces operations is hereby announced"** after a **"painful response,"** with a warning it would **resume if the IDF continues operations in southern Lebanon;** Israel halted its attacks (Netanyahu stopping short of acknowledging a ceasefire); and Trump posted that both are **"looking to do an immediate CEASEFIRE."** **Oil** spiked then faded — **Brent crossed ~$98 intraday June 8 then eased to ~$94** as Iran ended operations (WTI +4% above $94); **OPEC+ added 188,000 bpd for July;** **Hormuz traffic stayed near-zero (~36/week);** nuclear talks stayed **deadlocked over Iran's $24B frozen-assets demand.** **DPRK:** **Xi Jinping visited Pyongyang June 8-9** (2nd visit in 7 years) as **Kim ordered a 10,000-tonne destroyer** and "secret underwater weapons." **Russia-Ukraine:** static front; next Istanbul round June 20-30. **Venezuela:** Maduro+Flores next NYC court date June 30. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED:** the war's most extreme escalation to date stayed entirely in the **missile/air register** and **resolved toward de-escalation** — **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft** — and **decisively, the US was not the kinetic actor; Trump's single most consequential move was to RESTRAIN Israel** (open-questions: june3-kuwait-kinetic-escalation-2026; iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026).

> **Daily update — June 8, 2026 (Day 102).** The **Beirut tripwire was crossed** in the cycle's most serious escalation. On **Sunday June 7** Israel **struck Beirut's southern suburbs (Dahieh) without warning** — despite a US request to stand down — killing **2 and wounding ~20 (including 4 women and 4 children)** per Lebanon's Health Ministry, in what Netanyahu's office called retaliation for Hezbollah fire on northern Israel and a strike on Hezbollah **"command centers"** (CSMonitor; Washington Times; Al Jazeera, June 7). In response **Iran fired its FIRST direct missile barrage at Israel since the April 8 ceasefire** — directly actualizing FM **Araghchi's June 4 warning** that an Israeli Beirut strike would trigger a **"full-scale resumption."** Iran's state broadcaster confirmed the launches; Israel's **Iron Dome and Arrow** intercepted but the IDF said **"the defense is not hermetic,"** with sirens across the north — **casualty reporting diverged** (the IDF's initial statement reported **no immediate casualties**, while **NPR** described an Iranian **cluster-warhead missile striking a building in Ramat Gan, killing two residents in their 70s,** and **debris damaging the Old City of Jerusalem** near Al-Aqsa/Temple Mount, the Church of the Holy Sepulchre and the Jewish Quarter). IDF spokesman **Effie Defrin** said **"Iran has made a grave mistake";** chief of staff **Eyal Zamir** vowed to strike **"as soon as the order is given."** The **IRGC** warned any repeat would bring responses **"broader in scope"** encompassing **"all American and Zionist targets throughout the region,"** and Iran's **Foreign Ministry** called the **June 6 US strikes on radar sites at Goruk and Qeshm Island "a clear violation of the 8 April ceasefire and an act of military aggression"** — drawing the US, not only Israel, into the frame. **Trump pushed de-escalation:** he told Israel's Kan he did not think Israel needed to respond further, called the Beirut strike **"not coordinated with the US and I'm not happy about it,"** told Fox News **"you've shot your missiles, that's enough... get back to the table and make a deal,"** and said he would call Netanyahu to urge restraint (NPR; PBS; Axios; CBS News, June 7-8). **Nuclear talks stayed deadlocked over Iran's $24B frozen-assets demand** ($12B on signing + $12B within 60 days); **Grossi** said IAEA inspector access remains limited; mediation continued via **Pakistan, Qatar, Turkey and Egypt.** **Oil held near ~$94-97 Brent** (Brent fell below $94 on June 5; Chinese crude imports at a ~10-year low weighed on prices) and **did NOT spike** despite the exchange; **Hormuz traffic stayed near-zero (~36/week).** In **Russia-Ukraine**, the front stayed static (Russia unable to advance west of Pokrovsk since Dec 2025); next Istanbul round June 20-30. **DPRK:** Kim's June 3 new bomb-fuel plant stands. **Venezuela:** Maduro+Flores next NYC court date June 30. **Ground-invasion rung UNCHANGED:** the cycle's most serious escalation stayed in the **missile/air register** — **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft** — and the dominant US signal was **de-escalatory** (Trump urging both sides back to the table); the US was not the June 7 kinetic actor (open-questions: june3-kuwait-kinetic-escalation-2026; iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026).

> **Daily update — June 7, 2026 (Day 101).** The US-Iran exchange turned **kinetic a second time and widened to a second Gulf state.** On **June 6** CENTCOM said US forces **shot down four Iranian one-way attack drones** launched toward the Strait of Hormuz ("an immediate threat to regional maritime traffic") and **intercepted six of seven Iranian ballistic missiles fired at Kuwait AND Bahrain** (the seventh failed to reach its target); **Bahrain's Defense Ministry intercepted three missiles and a number of drones** and sounded air-raid sirens — the **first direct targeting of Bahrain** (the June 3 barrage hit Kuwait only). In response, US forces **struck Iranian coastal surveillance radar sites at Goruk and on Qeshm Island** "to defend against further attacks"; CENTCOM said forces "remain vigilant and postured to respond to unjustified Iranian aggression in self-defense," while **Iran's Mehr** framed the drones as **"warning shots"** tied to US ship movements (CENTCOM; Fox News; RFE/RL; Middle East Monitor, June 6, 2026). **Trump** said Iran's nuclear ambitions are **"finished,"** put Tehran at **"21-22%" of its pre-February missile arsenal,** and said he **"won't rush into a bad deal";** the US **sanctioned Iranian LNG/LPG-smuggling networks** (Fox News; The Statesman, June 6, 2026). **Talks remain deadlocked over the $24B frozen-assets demand;** Grossi still sees possible movement toward a preliminary framework. **Kuwait expelled two Iranian embassy staff** over the June 3 airport attack (Gulf News). In **Lebanon, Israeli strikes killed nine June 6, including three Lebanese Army officers** — a sharp escalation of the fraying June 4 ceasefire framework (Fox News; CNN; Al Jazeera). **Oil eased to ~$94.8 Brent** despite the flare-up (reduced Chinese demand a factor); **Hormuz traffic stayed near-zero (~36/week).** In Russia-Ukraine, the front stayed static (Russia unable to advance west of Pokrovsk since Dec 2025); next Istanbul round June 20-30. **North Korea** unveiled a new bomb-fuel plant (June 3) and vowed "exponential" nuclear expansion. **Ground-invasion rung unchanged:** the flare-up was **horizontal escalation** (a second Gulf state) within the **air/naval + missile/drone register**, with **no US troops in Iran, no Kharg assault, no new ARG/MEU order and no draft** (open-questions: june3-kuwait-kinetic-escalation-2026; iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026).

> **Daily update — June 6, 2026 (Day 100).** Iran hardened its public line while the negotiated 60-day MOU stayed unsigned. In an exclusive CNN interview in Tehran (June 5), **Mohsen Rezaei** — military adviser to Supreme Leader **Mojtaba Khamenei** — said **"the negotiations are at a deadlock and Trump must break this deadlock,"** that **"the ball is in Trump's court,"** and that a potential interim deal now hinges on the US releasing **$24 billion in frozen Iranian assets** ($12B on signing + $12B later) as **"a test of trust … America must pass";** he warned Iran would **"drag the war"** beyond the Persian Gulf and **"give another dimension to the war by attacking these other American bases"** if fighting resumed, said **"our land power is many times greater than our missiles,"** proposed Iran and Oman jointly manage Hormuz for **"maintenance fees"** rather than tolls, and called the war Iran's **"first triumph"** in 47 years (CNN; Newsmax; The Express Tribune, June 5, 2026). Separately, **Trump revealed he had considered but REJECTED a plan to send US troops into Iran to collect enriched-uranium "nuclear dust,"** saying it would have needed **"at least two weeks"** on the ground and airlifted **"massive equipment,"** that the material is **"entombed,"** and there was **"no reason to"** (ABC News live updates, June 5, 2026) — the clearest US-ground-option deliberation of the war, resolved AGAINST deployment. **IAEA's Grossi** saw possible movement toward a preliminary nuclear framework (obstacles: inspector access + verifying the **60% HEU** stockpile; options export/dilution/IAEA custody), and the **US sanctioned Iranian oil/LPG-smuggling networks.** In **Lebanon**, President **Aoun** accused Iran of **"using Lebanon as a bargaining chip,"** Hezbollah's **Qassem** called the June 4 framework an **"imaginary ceasefire"** that would amount to **"surrender,"** four were killed June 5 in Israeli strikes on **Nabatieh and Bint Jbeil,** the IDF ordered evacuations in **≥9 towns** and said it killed Hezbollah engineering-unit commander **Abed Harb,** France called a **UN Security Council emergency session,** and the toll reached **~3,500 killed** since March 2. **Oil:** Brent held the **mid-$90s (~$95.25, +0.23% June 5)**; **Hormuz ~7 ships Friday + 4 over the weekend (~36/week)** vs ~100-130/day pre-war (4-6 months to normalize). In **Russia-Ukraine, Lavrov reaffirmed the April 2022 Istanbul draft** as Moscow's basis; Russia netted +1,427 sq mi over the year but a net LOSS in April; next Istanbul round June 20-30. **Latin America:** CIA Director **Ratcliffe visited Havana,** bringing a paramilitary leader from the Maduro-capture op; Maduro + Flores next NYC court date June 30. **Ground-invasion rung unchanged / deescalatory:** Trump publicly rejected a US-ground uranium-recovery mission; Rezaei's "wider war" / "land power" lines are Iranian retaliatory/defensive rhetoric in a missile/base register, not a US-ground trigger; and there were **no new US troop movements, no third ARG, no new MEU order and no draft** (open-questions: iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026; june3-kuwait-kinetic-escalation-2026).

> **Daily update — June 5, 2026 (Day 99).** The Lebanon track moved to center stage. After the June 2-3 Washington trilateral talks, the **US State Department announced June 4 an Israel-Lebanon CONDITIONAL ceasefire framework** — "contingent on a complete cessation" of Hezbollah fire and the evacuation of all Hezbollah operatives **south of the Litani**, with "**pilot**" security zones under exclusive **Lebanese Armed Forces** control — but **Hezbollah's Naim Kassem rejected it** as "absurd, humiliating and insulting" (leaving the south under fire would mean "surrender, defeat"), demanded a **full Israeli withdrawal**, and **Israel and Hezbollah traded fresh strikes**; Lebanese **President Aoun** called the deal the "**last chance**" for a comprehensive truce (NPR; Al Jazeera; NBC; TIME; Washington Post, June 4, 2026). The Lebanon track is pivotal because **Iran tied its June 1 suspension of US talks to Israel halting its Lebanon offensive.** On the US-Iran track the same-day messaging **diverged sharply**: Iranian FM **Araghchi said "no tangible progress"** had been made and warned that **any Israeli attack on Beirut would trigger a "full-scale resumption"** of the US-Iran conflict, while **VP Vance called a deal "very close" but "not there yet"** (still "going back and forth" on the HEU stockpile + enrichment; Iran "negotiating... in good faith") and **Trump said Iran had "agreed not to pursue a nuclear weapon,"** floating a possible meeting with **Mojtaba Khamenei** "if developments continue"; the **IAEA urged Tehran to engage** on its nuclear material (CBS; The Hill; RFE/RL; Fortune, June 4, 2026). **Pakistan's Foreign Office** "categorically and unequivocally" **rejected claims** (by ex-CIA analyst Larry Johnson) that FM **Dar shared Iran nuclear intelligence with Rubio** at their May 29 meeting; Rubio: "not aware of any such message" (Dawn; Pakistan Today, June 4, 2026). **Oil**: Brent eased to **~$97.95/bbl** (June 4 AM, down ~$3.41 from the June 3 flare-up high) before ticking back above $97 on Trump's nuclear comment; **Hormuz traffic ~36 ships in the seven days to Friday** vs ~130/day pre-war. In Russia-Ukraine, **Russia still claims full Luhansk** (Kyiv denies) ahead of US-envoy talks; next Istanbul round June 20-30. **Ground-invasion rung unchanged / deescalatory:** the Lebanon ceasefire framework points toward halting the front Iran named as its precondition, Araghchi's "full-scale resumption" warning is a **conditional missile/air-register tripwire** keyed to an Israeli Beirut strike (not a US-ground trigger), and there were **no new US troop movements, no third ARG, no new MEU order, no draft** (open-questions: iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026; june3-kuwait-kinetic-escalation-2026).

> **Daily update — June 4, 2026 (Day 98).** The deal-or-collapse standoff turned **kinetic**. Overnight June 2-3, after an MQ-1 downing and a June 2 CENTCOM **Hellfire that disabled a Kharg-bound tanker**, US forces struck **two Iranian command-and-control sites near Hormuz** (reported at **Goruk and Qeshm Island**); Iran said the US had hit **"Kharg and Abu Musa"** islands and a vessel, and the **IRGC claimed it struck a US airbase** ("all predetermined targets destroyed"). At dawn June 3 **Iran fired 13 ballistic missiles and 17 drones** at Gulf targets; Kuwait intercepted them, but **falling debris hit Kuwait International Airport, killing 1 and wounding 63** and badly damaging the terminal a day after it reopened (Kuwait Airways, Emirates, IndiGo suspended/diverted) (NBC; CBS; The National, June 3, 2026). Despite the exchange, **Trump and Rubio said talks continued** and **VP Vance (June 4) called the US and Iran "very close" but "not there yet"** on the 60-day MOU, still "going back and forth on the nuclear stuff"; **Trump** demanded Hormuz reopen and Iran's HEU be **"unearthed by the United States" and "destroyed,"** while **Ghalibaf** said Iran wins **"concessions not through talks but through missiles"** (CNBC; The Hill, June 2-4, 2026). Domestically, the **House passed a war-powers resolution 215-208 (June 3)** directing Trump to end Iran hostilities (four Republicans crossing over; war cost cited >$100B; Senate passage unlikely, veto likely) (CNN; NPR). Iran widened its coercion to a **second chokepoint** as Quds Force chief **Qa'ani warned the Bab al-Mandeb** would face Hormuz-like restrictions (GlobalSecurity.org, June 2). In Lebanon, a **June 1 Israel-Hezbollah understanding** framed the fourth-round State Department talks, but **IDF chief Zamir said "there is no ceasefire for our forces"** and Netanyahu kept southern-Lebanon strikes "as planned" (Times of Israel, June 3). **Oil** rose >3% into the high-$90s/~$100. In Russia-Ukraine, **Russia claimed full control of Luhansk** (Kyiv denied) ahead of US-envoy talks; next Istanbul round proposed June 20-30. **Ground-invasion rung unchanged despite the kinetic spike:** every US action was air/naval (island + C2 strikes, a Hellfire on a tanker) and every Iranian action missile/drone — still zero US ground troops in Iran, no Kharg ground assault, no new ARG/MEU order, no draft; the named deal-failure alternative (air/strike package) is now being **partly exercised** rather than replaced by a ground order, and the House war-powers vote cuts against escalation (open-questions: june3-kuwait-kinetic-escalation-2026; iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026).

> **Daily update — June 3, 2026 (Day 97).** The cycle was dominated by **Iran's June 1 suspension of its mediator-routed talks with the US**, which Tehran's negotiating team tied to "the continuation of the Zionist regime's crimes in Lebanon" and conditioned on Israel halting its southern-Lebanon offensive (NPR; CNN, June 1, 2026). **Trump insisted the deal was still alive** — telling CNBC he "did not care" if the talks were over and that they had "started to get very boring," urging the public to "just sit back and relax," claiming a "very productive call" with Netanyahu and (via intermediaries) Hezbollah, and saying a deal was reachable "over the next week" and that there would be **"no troops going to Beirut"** — even as CNN reported a heated, expletive-laden Trump-Netanyahu exchange. **Netanyahu said Lebanon strikes would continue "as planned"** and Katz denied any Lebanon ceasefire; Iran's chief negotiator **Ghalibaf warned of "direct confrontation,"** and the **IRGC's Hormuz-shipping threat stood** (Iran "preparing to fully close" the Strait; Lloyd's List: closed since May 4). In Lebanon, the **fourth round of US-brokered Israel-Lebanon talks ran at the State Department June 2-3** (US side led by Deputy NSA Mike Needham, State Dept Chief of Staff Dan Holler, Amb. Mike Huckabee, with the Pentagon now seated) on ceasefire enforcement and a "move versus move" mechanism; one Israeli soldier was killed June 2 near Beaufort, and Lebanon's toll reached ~3,370+ killed (Wikipedia; The Washington Institute; Times of Israel). In Russia-Ukraine, the **June 2 Istanbul round ended quickly** — a new 1,000-1,200-for-1,000-1,200 POW swap, a 6,000-body exchange and a wounded-exchange commission agreed, but no ceasefire; next round proposed June 20-30 (Kyiv Independent; PBS NewsHour). **Oil** rose to ~$95/bbl on the suspension before easing. **Ground-invasion rung unchanged:** still zero US ground troops in Iran; Trump explicitly ruled out troops to Beirut; the suspension raises deal-collapse risk but the named deal-failure alternative stays an air/strike package, not a ground order; the Lebanon track is Israeli ground operations plus US diplomacy, not US ground troops in Iran (open-questions: iran-suspends-us-talks-lebanon-june-2026).
>
> **Daily update — June 2, 2026 (Day 96).** At the "turn of the week" the negotiated **60-day US-Iran MOU remained UNSIGNED**: Trump's May 31 requested edits (HEU hand-over specifics/timing + Hormuz-reopening wording) carried into the new week without a signature, and the disputes widened — Iranian sources say the draft contains "no such clause" on a toll-free Strait and "no reference to Iran dismantling or destroying its nuclear materials," while Iranian state media reframed the immediate payment of **$12 billion** of Iran's frozen assets as "the most important part of the agreement" and a precondition for further talks (CNN; CBS News, May 31, 2026). In Lebanon, **Israel-Lebanon indirect talks reconvene at the US State Department June 2-3** (second round after the May 14-15 Washington meeting that produced a 45-day ceasefire extension) even as Netanyahu — calling the Beaufort Castle capture a "dramatic turning point" — ordered strikes on Hezbollah in Beirut (Al Jazeera; The Washington Times). In Russia-Ukraine, a **second direct round was held June 2 in Istanbul** — memoranda exchanged, a new prisoner swap agreed, no ceasefire, positions far apart (Kyiv Independent; RFE/RL). **Ground-invasion rung unchanged:** still zero US ground troops in Iran; the Beirut strikes, Beaufort capture, and June 2-3 State Department track are Israeli ground operations and US diplomacy, not US ground troops in Iran.

## Quick Reference: Disconfirmed Claims

| Claim | Reality |
|-------|---------|
| "America will never shut out Chinese students" | Trump admin aggressively revoking visas (May 2025) |
| "US-China rapprochement" | SCOTUS struck down IEEPA tariffs Feb 20; Trump imposed 10% global tariff under Section 122; China tariffs at 47%; truce extended but tensions remain; Trump-Xi summit May 14-15 |
| "Germany-Russia rapprochement" | Relations frozen; economic decoupling; policy explicitly rejects rapprochement |
| "Russia as nuclear guarantor preventing strikes on Iran" | Russia did NOT prevent strikes; treaty lacks mutual defense clause |
| "Coalition includes Saudi Arabia" | Saudi refused airspace, condemned strikes on Iran |
| Trump picks Nikki Haley as VP | Trump chose JD Vance |

Araghchi LEFT Islamabad BEFORE Witkoff/Kushner had even arrived — chronology means Iran's posture is 'we delivered our demands; you respond,' not 'we walked out' (Iran International, Press TV, WANA, Times of Israel, Tribune, ANI, Pravda USA, Muscat Daily)

First public official acknowledgment of post-blockade strain on Iranian energy. Iran's president framing war as a domestic-economic-resilience challenge rather than a kinetic-mobilization moment is Tehran's strategic-posture statement (RedState, AAWSAT, Times of Israel, Dunya News)
- NETANYAHU ORDERS IDF TO STRIKE HEZBOLLAH 'WITH FORCE' — LEBANON CEASEFIRE FRAYING DAY 2 OF EXTENSION (Apr 25): Hezbollah fired 2 rockets + drones at northern Israel; sirens in Manara, Margaliot, Misgav Am; Hezbollah explosive drones detonated 'adjacent to' IDF soldiers south of Forward Defense Line. IDF struck rocket launchers in Yater + Kafra; artillery on Houla. Lebanese Health Ministry: 6 killed in Israeli strikes Saturday. Hezbollah second ceasefire violation since Apr 16. 15+ Lebanese killed since ceasefire. Netanyahu's response stays AIR-STRIKE register, not ground-widening. Iran's senior voices STILL deliberately decoupling from Lebanon track (Haaretz, JPost, Times of Israel, Detroit News, WHBL, Press TV, CBC, Tribune India, Business Standard)
- RUSSIA-UKRAINE APR 25 — ONE OF LARGEST AERIAL STRIKES OF WAR: 47 missiles + 619 drones; 580 drones + 30 missiles intercepted. 7 KILLED, 57+ INJURED. Dnipro hardest hit (10+ hour attack); Chernihiv 2 killed. Russia total combat losses ~1,324,690 (+1,230 from Apr 24). Pentagon four-pressure-point posture intensifies — strain against any Iran ground theater opening (Kyiv Independent, Euronews, US News, PBS, Moscow Times)
- WAR POWERS 60-DAY DEADLINE APR 29 / STATUTORY MAY 1: Per 1973 War Powers Resolution, US must terminate military operations after 60 days unless Congress votes on AUMF. Sens Murkowski (AK), Tillis (NC), Collins (ME), Curtis (UT) want vote. SML Thune + SFRC chair Risch decline to schedule. House previously rejected withdrawal effort. Conservative read: complete absence of AUMF debate three days from 60-day mark structurally confirms ground operations not on operational horizon (Foreign Policy, CNN, The Hill, Time, ABC News, PBS NewsHour, Responsible Statecraft)
- TRUMP 'SHOOT AND KILL' DIRECTIVE ON MINE-LAYING BOATS + 'TOTAL CONTROL... SEALED UP TIGHT' RHETORIC (Apr 23-24): Trump Truth Social ordered US Navy 'to shoot and kill any boat' laying mines in Hormuz and tripled minesweeping effort ('There is to be no hesitation'). Later: 'We have total control over the Strait of Hormuz. No ship can enter or leave without the approval of the United States Navy. It is Sealed up Tight, until such time as Iran is able to make a DEAL!!!' IRGC called the directive 'an overt breach of the ceasefire.' FIRST EXPLICIT US KINETIC-USE-OF-FORCE DIRECTIVE — narrowly scoped to mine-layers, maritime-domain only, not a ground authorization (CNBC, WashPost, NBC, Al Jazeera, Euronews, CBS)
- US SEIZES THIRD TANKER M/T MAJESTIC X (GUYANA FLAG, EX-'PHONIX') (Apr 23): US helicopter-fast-rope-VBSS boarded the Guyana-flagged Majestic X (formerly 'Phonix', OFAC-sanctioned 2024) in Indian Ocean. DoD released boarding video. Second Indo-Pacific-Command-area seizure since ceasefire extension Apr 22. Invoked 'right-of-visit.' Seizure scoreboard 3-2 US-Iran; all 5 operations boarding-class (WashPost, Reuters, AP, ABC, Just The News, OANN, Pentagon)
- USS GEORGE H.W. BUSH ARRIVES CENTCOM AOR — THIRD US CARRIER IN THEATRE (Apr 23): CENTCOM announced USS Bush arrival in Indian Ocean after Africa transit. Three-carrier posture: Ford (Red Sea, 301+ days), Lincoln (Arabian Sea), Bush (Indian Ocean). 17+ warships + 100+ aircraft + 10,000+ blockade + 50,000+ CENTCOM. Strike-projection surge, NOT ground-projection (no third ARG) (DNYUZ/Korea Times, Middle East Eye, Antiwar, Times of Israel, CENTCOM)
- ISRAEL-LEBANON CEASEFIRE EXTENDED 3 WEEKS (Apr 23): Trump Oval Office amb-level second round with Israel's Leiter + Lebanon's Moawad. Original Apr 26 expiry → ~May 17. Iran's primary public precondition for talks FORMALLY RESTORED. Proxy-activation pathway re-bounded (CNBC, CBS, Axios, Reuters, Al Jazeera, CBC, MPR News, WashPost)
- AMAL KHALIL — AL-AKHBAR JOURNALIST KILLED IN ISRAELI 'DOUBLE-TAP' STRIKE (Apr 22-23): First strike on car, second strike on house where Khalil and photographer Zeinab Faraj sheltered in at-Tiri. Khalil trapped alive under rubble; Red Cross blocked by Israeli fire 7hrs; dead on arrival. 5 Lebanese killed Apr 22 despite ceasefire. CPJ calls for international investigation; Lebanese PM war-crime allegations (Al Jazeera, WashPost, Democracy Now, CNN, CPJ, New Arab)

Protester doused him with red liquid (tomato juice); detained by police. - IRAN RETALIATES OPERATIONALLY — IRGC SEIZES MSC FRANCESCA + EPAMINONDAS IN STRAIT (Apr 22-23): IRGC Navy boarded both vessels in coordinated retaliation for Apr 19 Touska + Apr 21 Tifani interdictions. MSC Francesca (Panama, MSC-operated, formerly 'Persian Gulf'); Epaminondas (Liberia, Greek-owned via Technomar/Youroukos, MSC charter, fired on with small-arms+RPGs causing heavy bridge damage). Both escorted to Sirik. IRGC framing emphasizes navigation/permit irregularities — deliberate legalistic mirror of US 'sanctions enforcement' framing. Iran chose narrowest available option: maritime mirror, not cross-domain (UKMTO, Reuters, Lloyd's List, Maritime Executive, Tasnim, Iran International, Press TV, Al Jazeera)
- HEZBOLLAH BREAKS LEBANON CEASEFIRE — FIRST KINETIC EXCHANGE SINCE APR 16/17 (Apr 22-23): Rockets + drones at IDF positions in Rab Thalathin; sirens in Kfar Yuval and Ma'ayan Baruch. Apr 18-22 Yellow Line demolitions + Apr 22 Fadlallah vow operationalized. Iran's primary public precondition for talks no longer nominally intact. Critically: none of Iran's senior voices claimed Hezbollah action as Iranian retaliation — Tehran deliberately decoupling from Lebanon (Times of Israel, Al-Akhbar, Haaretz, Reuters, Al Jazeera)
- IRAN UN 'PIRACY' FILING (Apr 22-23): UN Ambassador Iravani submitted formal letter to UN Secretary-General characterizing Touska seizure as 'maritime piracy' and violation of international law. Investment in UN paperwork is the inverse of mobilization posture (UN press, Iran MFA, Tasnim, Press TV, IRNA)
- TRUMP 'NO TIME FRAME' + REPORTED 3-5 DAY INFORMAL WINDOW (Apr 22-23): Trump told reporters there is 'no time frame' on indefinite ceasefire extension; denied 2026 midterms factor. - IRAN REJECTS TRUMP EXECUTIONS CLAIM AS 'FALSE NEWS' (Apr 22-23): Trump publicly thanked Iran for 'scrapping' executions of 8 women. Iran's judiciary called the claim 'false news.' Iran Human Rights NGO confirmed 2 of 8 women already on bail; the other 6 face capital charges but no execution dates were ever set. Building wins for diplomatic track is inverse of ground-mobilization posture (Iran International, IranWire, Iran Human Rights, Reuters)
- PEZESHKIAN + GHALIBAF HARDEN POSITION IN MARITIME REGISTER (Apr 22-23): Pezeshkian: 'Breach of commitments, blockade and threats are the main obstacles to genuine negotiations.' Tehran's executive + legislative leaders aligned with FM Araghchi's hard line — but framing stays naval-domain (IRNA, Tasnim, ISNA, Press TV)
- THE WEEK INDIA 'GROUND INVASION QUITE LIKELY' (Apr 22-23): First analyst-class outlet to use ground-invasion language in serious commentary. Article's argument rests on Iranian INFERENCE about US intent, NOT on observable US ground-deployment indicators. Logged for honesty; tracker absolute-zero unchanged (The Week India)
- TRUMP EXTENDS CEASEFIRE INDEFINITELY (Apr 22): Truth Social post: ceasefire extended 'until such time as' Iran's leaders submit a 'unified proposal' to end the war. Cited Iran's 'seriously fractured' government. Acknowledged PM Shehbaz Sharif + Field Marshal Asim Munir requested the pause. Blockade remains in force; US military 'ready and able.' Reverses Apr 21 morning 'highly unlikely' stance (CBS, NBC, CNN, Axios, NPR, Time, Al Jazeera)

- SECOND SHIP INTERDICTED — M/T TIFANI IN BAY OF BENGAL (Apr 21): Pentagon confirmed US forces boarded stateless sanctioned tanker M/T Tifani 'without incident' via helicopter fast-rope. Pentagon video shows service members rappelling onto deck. Tanker carrying Iranian oil bound for China. Geographic expansion ~3,000nm east of Hormuz. SECOND observed US boarding in 3 days, both via helicopter fast-rope = VBSS doctrinal pattern confirmed, NOT amphibious assault (Stars and Stripes, Washington Times, Fox News, US News, PBS, Military.com, Manila Times, Al Jazeera)
- VANCE ISLAMABAD TRIP CALLED OFF — WITKOFF + KUSHNER ALSO STAYED (Apr 22): VP remained at White House Tuesday; Witkoff + Kushner also did not depart. US delegation now 'TBD.' Notable: cancellation NOT substituted with military delegation (Defense Secretary, CENTCOM commander, joint chiefs all absent). Pre-invasion pattern would predict diplomatic-to-military substitution; observed pattern is full diplomatic suspension (Fox News, ABC, NPR, Axios)
- IRAN NOTIFIES PAKISTAN: NO WEDNESDAY DELEGATION — ARAGHCHI 'ACT OF WAR' (Apr 22): Tasnim reports Iran informed Pakistani mediators it would not send a delegation for Wednesday talks. FM Araghchi: blockade constitutes 'an act of war' and ceasefire violation; Iran won't negotiate 'under the shadow of threats.' Iranian rhetoric hardens but stays in MARITIME-DOMAIN register — 'act of war' deployed against blockade specifically, not reframed as ground-mobilization call (Tasnim, Al Jazeera, NBC, CNBC, Euronews)
- IRAN CLAIMS DRONE ATTACKS ON US WARSHIPS — UNCONFIRMED (Apr 21-22): Tasnim and Iran International reported Iranian drones struck US vessels in retaliation for Touska seizure — no damage reported, no US confirmation. Misbar factcheck noted circulated video appeared outdated. IF verified, first kinetic Iranian retaliation — still MARITIME-DOMAIN (US warships). Doctrinal pattern holds either way: Iran retaliation channel naval-tit-for-tat (WION, Iran International, Business Today, Tasnim, Misbar)
- KHATAM AL-ANBIYA: 'READY TO GIVE DECISIVE RESPONSE' (Apr 21): Iran commander: 'We are ready to give a decisive response to the enemy's breach of covenant.' Rhetorical reiteration of Apr 19 retaliation vow; Apr 20 family-hostage deferral framing preserved (ANI News)
- CENTCOM RELEASES TOUSKA BOARDING VIDEO (Apr 20): 33-second clip shows Marines fast-roping from MH-60S Seahawk helicopters launched from USS Tripoli (LHA-7) onto Touska's deck — confirms VBSS (Visit, Board, Search, and Seizure) doctrine. With Tifani boarding Apr 21, a pattern-of-two is established: blockade enforcement, NOT amphibious-assault doctrine (CENTCOM, USNI News, The Hill, Reuters)
- IRAN KHATAM AL-ANBIYA DEFERRED RETALIATION CITING CREW FAMILIES (Apr 20): 'delayed due to the presence of family members of the ship's crew on board, to protect their lives.' Face-saving deferral preserves rhetorical commitment without immediate kinetic action. Stated retaliation pathway remains MARITIME (Tasnim, Tabnak, Reuters, Iran International, Al Jazeera)
- TRUMP THREATENED 'KNOCK OUT EVERY POWER PLANT AND BRIDGE' (Apr 20-21, now DEFERRED via indefinite extension): 'we'll have to knock out every single Power Plant, and every single Bridge, in Iran' if no deal. FIRST EXPLICIT NAMING of civilian-dual-use infrastructure as US targets. Reescalation language was AIR-CAMPAIGN ('knock out' = bombing) — canonical air-campaign target list (Op Allied Force, Op Desert Storm), NOT ground-invasion target list. Threat paused by Apr 22 indefinite extension (CBS News, Reuters, Bloomberg, ABC News)
- 4 US GOV'T AIRCRAFT AT NUR KHAN AIR BASE (Apr 19-20): Flight tracking: 4 US aircraft (comms equipment + motorcade support) landed Sunday at Nur Khan in Rawalpindi. Pre-positioning for rescheduled Witkoff/Kushner delegation; Apr 22 trip cancellation leaves them as standby for rescheduled mission. Cargo composition is diplomatic-mission signature, not force-projection signature (The Hindu, Geo News, OSINT)
- USS SPRUANCE SEIZES IRANIAN TOUSKA (Apr 19): DDG-111 fired multiple 5-inch MK 45 rounds into engine room after 6 hours of warnings. Marines boarded and took custody. Touska transiting at 17 knots en route to Bandar Abbas. Under US Treasury sanctions. FIRST BOARDING AND FIRST VESSEL FIRED UPON of blockade (CENTCOM, CNBC, CNN, NPR, Axios, The National)
- IRAN REJECTS APR 21 TALKS VIA IRNA (Apr 19): Official IRNA: Iranian officials not participating in Islamabad talks, citing 'Washington's excessive demands, unrealistic expectations, constant shifts in stance, repeated contradictions and the ongoing naval blockade.' Iran softened hours later — 'talks continue while Iran retains control of Hormuz.' Decision not formalized (IRNA, CNBC, Al Jazeera, Irish Times, ABC News)
- ZERO-TANKER DAY APR 19: No tankers transited Hormuz Apr 19; ~230 loaded oil tankers still waiting. Commercial transit halted back to pre-Apr 17 conditions. Iran's Apr 17 'completely open' declaration fully reversed (BBC, UKMTO, NBC)
- OIL EXTENDED REBOUND (Apr 20): WTI closed Apr 20 at $88.80/bbl (+5%+ from Apr 17 $83.85 close). Brent ~$94+ tracking. Tuesday trading absorbing Trump 'Power Plants/Bridges' threat. Near $100 territory remains in play heading into Apr 22 ceasefire expiry (CNBC, Reuters, Bloomberg, Fortune)
- IRAN RECLOSES STRAIT OF HORMUZ (Apr 18): SNSC reversed Apr 17 reopening citing US "non-reciprocation" — blockade had continued despite Iran's voluntary opening. Passage will be "strictly managed" until US lifts naval blockade. Reverses single most consequential concession of ceasefire period (IRNA, Tasnim, Reuters, Al Jazeera, NYT)
- IRGC FIRES ON INDIAN-FLAGGED TANKERS (Apr 18): IRGC fast attack craft engaged VLCC Sanmar Herald (~2M bbl Iraqi crude) and Jag Arnav ~20nm NE of Oman. India MEA summoned Iran Ambassador Mohammad Fathali in "strongest possible terms" — first overt Indian diplomatic protest of war (Reuters, Hindustan Times, MEA, gCaptain, Bloomberg)
- IRAN RECLOSES STRAIT OF HORMUZ (Apr 18): SNSC reversed Apr 17 reopening citing US "non-reciprocation" — blockade had continued despite Iran's voluntary opening. India MEA summoned Iran Ambassador Mohammad Fathali in "strongest possible terms" — first overt Indian diplomatic protest of war (Reuters, Hindustan Times, MEA, gCaptain, Bloomberg)

Tied to Lebanon ceasefire. Ships must use Iranian-designated routes 'in coordination with Iranian authorities.' Iran voluntarily relinquishing central leverage chip — most consequential concession since ceasefire (NBC, NYT, BBC, Reuters, PBS, Politico, CBS)

Minutes later: US blockade 'WILL CONTINUE UNTIL SUCH TIME AS OUR TRANSACTION WITH IRAN IS 100% COMPLETE' — 'as soon as the agreement gets signed, that's when the blockade ends' (Politico, NYT, CBS, Reuters)
- SECOND ROUND CONFIRMED MONDAY APR 21 ISLAMABAD (Apr 17-18): Pakistan-brokered. Both delegations arriving Sunday. VP Vance expected to lead US delegation. Pakistan FM Ishaq Dar: agreement 'more than 80 percent complete' but requires 'flexibility from both sides' (Dawn, Al Jazeera, The News Pakistan, Bloomberg, ARY News)
- OIL CRASHES 9-11% (Apr 17): Brent -9.07% to $90.38/bbl; WTI -11.45% to $83.85/bbl — second-largest daily drop of entire war. S&P 500 record high; Dow +869 (+1.79%); Nasdaq +2.12%. Markets pricing Apr 21 deal success (NYT, CNBC, TradingEconomics, Bloomberg, WSJ)
- FIRST CRUISE SHIP TRANSITS HORMUZ (Apr 17): Celestyal Discovery (Malta-registered) — first passenger vessel through Strait of Hormuz since war began; stranded 47 days in Dubai. BBC tracking: 'few ships moving' — commercial traffic unlikely to return to pre-war levels immediately (BBC, Reuters, Travel Weekly)

Araghchi: 'inches away' from deal when Iran 'encountered maximalism, shifting goalposts, and blockade.' Accused US of trying 'to achieve at the negotiating table what it could not achieve through war' (WaPo, BBC, Al Jazeera, Tehran Times, Al Mayadeen)
- ISRAEL-LEBANON CEASEFIRE HOLDS DAY 5 — YELLOW LINE DEMOLITIONS ESCALATE (Apr 21): Haaretz reports paid contractors operating heavy machinery have begun demolishing homes, public buildings, and schools in the Yellow Line zone — official 'clear the area' policy. Israeli army has stopped people returning to a dozen+ villages, expanding the no-go zone. Netanyahu's announced 'ten kilometres deep' security buffer being executed as physical destruction of civilian infrastructure. Hezbollah leader Naim Qassem vows retaliation but has NOT resumed kinetic exchanges. Structural ceasefire intact through Apr 26 expiry (Haaretz, Al Jazeera, Antiwar.com, aawsat.com, Wikipedia)

- USS BUSH STILL CIRCLING AFRICA off Namibia (avoiding Suez); USS FORD AT 300 DAYS — POST-VIETNAM RECORD FOR LONGEST CONTINUOUS US CARRIER DEPLOYMENT STILL EXTENDING. Signals strain, not surge (USNI, Stars and Stripes, The Hill, Breaking Defense, CENTCOM)
